3: from django.core.management.base import BaseCommand
4: from django.core.management import call_command
5: from django.utils import timezone
6: 
7: class Command(BaseCommand):
8:     help = 'Runs local background automation scheduler (Broadcasts, Fee Reminders, Hold 3-Day Grace Period, To-Do Auto-Trash, Visitor Follow-ups).'
9: 
10:     def add_arguments(self, parser):
11:         parser.add_argument('--once', action='store_true', help='Run all background tasks once and exit.')
12:         parser.add_argument('--interval', type=int, default=60, help='Loop interval in seconds (default: 60s).')
13: 
14:     def handle(self, *args, **options):
15:         run_once = options['once']
16:         interval = options['interval']
17: 
18:         self.stdout.write(self.style.SUCCESS("=================================================="))
19:         self.stdout.write(self.style.SUCCESS(">>> ABCD Local Real-Time Background Scheduler Active"))
20:         self.stdout.write(self.style.SUCCESS("=================================================="))
21:         self.stdout.write(f"Interval: {interval}s | Run Once Mode: {run_once}")
22: 
23:         last_daily_run = None
24: 
25:         try:
26:             while True:
27:                 now = timezone.localtime(timezone.now())
28:                 today_date = now.date()
29:                 self.stdout.write(f"\n[{now.strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S IST')}] Running high-frequency tasks...")
30: 
31:                 # 1. High Frequency Tasks (Broadcasts, To-Do Auto-Trash, Learning Reminders)
32:                 try:
33:                     call_command('run_scheduled_broadcasts')
34:                 except Exception as e:
35:                     self.stderr.write(f"Error running broadcasts: {e}")
36: 
37:                 try:
38:                     call_command('process_todo')
39:                 except Exception as e:
40:                     self.stderr.write(f"Error processing to-do & learning reminders: {e}")
41: 
42:                 # 2. Daily Tasks (Fee Reminders, Seat Hold Grace & Auto-Promotion, Visitor Expiry, Cleanup)
43:                 if last_daily_run != today_date or run_once:
44:                     self.stdout.write(self.style.NOTICE(f"[{today_date}] Executing Daily Maintenance Tasks..."))
45: 
46:                     try:
47:                         self.stdout.write(" -> Checking Seat Hold Grace Periods & Auto-Promotions...")
48:                         call_command('process_seat_reminders')
49:                     except Exception as e:
50:                         self.stderr.write(f"Error processing seat reminders: {e}")
51: 
52:                     try:
53:                         self.stdout.write(" -> Dispatching Fee Expiry Reminders & Overdue Warnings...")
54:                         call_command('send_fee_reminders')
55:                     except Exception as e:
56:                         self.stderr.write(f"Error sending fee reminders: {e}")
57: 
58:                     try:
59:                         self.stdout.write(" -> Checking Visitor Intents & Seat Waitlists...")
60:                         call_command('process_visitor_reminders')
61:                     except Exception as e:
62:                         self.stderr.write(f"Error processing visitor reminders: {e}")
63: 
64:                     try:
65:                         self.stdout.write(" -> Checking Birthday Wishes for Students & Alumni...")
66:                         from users.utils import process_birthday_wishes
67:                         process_birthday_wishes()
68:                     except Exception as e:
69:                         self.stderr.write(f"Error processing birthday wishes: {e}")
70: 
71:                     try:
72:                         self.stdout.write(" -> Cleaning up read notifications...")
73:                         call_command('cleanup_notifications')
74:                     except Exception as e:
75:                         self.stderr.write(f"Error cleaning notifications: {e}")
76: 
77:                     try:
78:                         self.stdout.write(" -> Cleaning up resolved complaint images (5d+)...")
79:                         call_command('cleanup_complaint_images')
80:                     except Exception as e:
81:                         self.stderr.write(f"Error cleaning complaint images: {e}")
82: 
83:                     try:
84:                         self.stdout.write(" -> Cleaning up old broadcasts & media (20d+)...")
85:                         call_command('cleanup_broadcasts')
86:                     except Exception as e:
87:                         self.stderr.write(f"Error cleaning broadcasts: {e}")
88: 
89:                     try:
90:                         self.stdout.write(" -> Purging expired chat media (10d+)...")
91:                         from users.views import purge_expired_media
92:                         purge_expired_media()
93:                     except Exception as e:
94:                         self.stderr.write(f"Error purging expired chat media: {e}")
95: 
96:                     try:
97:                         self.stdout.write(" -> Purging expired deleted group chats (30d+)...")
98:                         from users.views import purge_expired_group_chats
99:                         purge_expired_group_chats()
100:                     except Exception as e:
101:                         self.stderr.write(f"Error purging expired group chats: {e}")
102: 
103: 
104:                     last_daily_run = today_date
105:                     self.stdout.write(self.style.SUCCESS("[Daily Maintenance Complete]"))
106: 
107:                 if run_once:
108:                     self.stdout.write(self.style.SUCCESS("\n[Finished single-run execution]"))
109:                     break
110: 
111:                 self.stdout.write(f"Waiting {interval} seconds for next tick... (Press Ctrl+C to stop)")
112:                 time.sleep(interval)
113:         except KeyboardInterrupt:
114:             self.stdout.write(self.style.SUCCESS("\n[Scheduler stopped by user (Ctrl+C). Exiting...]"))
115: 
116: 
117:

+# ==============================================================================
+# SEO: ROBOTS.TXT & DYNAMIC XML SITEMAP
+# ==============================================================================
+
+def robots_txt_view(request):
+    """
+    SEO robots.txt view allowing search engine indexation of public pages
+    while protecting authenticated and administrative routes.
+    """
+    site_url = getattr(settings, 'SITE_URL', 'https://abcd2013.online').rstrip('/')
+    lines = [
+        "User-agent: *",
+        "Allow: /",
+        "Allow: /about/",
+        "Allow: /services/",
+        "Allow: /contact/",
+        "Allow: /courses/",
+        "Allow: /admission-form/",
+        "Allow: /library-availability/",
+        "Allow: /hall-of-fame/",
+        "Allow: /resolved-complaints/",
+        "Allow: /static/",
+        "",
+        "Disallow: /admin/",
+        "Disallow: /teacher/",
+        "Disallow: /dashboard/",
+        "Disallow: /alumni/",
+        "Disallow: /todo/",
+        "Disallow: /guidy/",
+        "Disallow: /api/",
+        "Disallow: /auth/",
+        "Disallow: /post-login/",
+        "",
+        f"Sitemap: {site_url}/sitemap.xml",
+    ]
+    return HttpResponse("\n".join(lines), content_type="text/plain")
+
+
+def sitemap_xml_view(request):
+    """
+    Dynamic XML Sitemap generating clean URLs for search engine discovery.
+    """
+    from django.utils import timezone
+    from users.models import Course
+
+    site_url = getattr(settings, 'SITE_URL', 'https://abcd2013.online').rstrip('/')
+    now_str = timezone.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d')
+
+    # Static public routes
+    static_urls = [
+        {'loc': f"{site_url}/", 'priority': '1.0', 'changefreq': 'daily'},
+        {'loc': f"{site_url}/about/", 'priority': '0.8', 'changefreq': 'monthly'},
+        {'loc': f"{site_url}/services/", 'priority': '0.8', 'changefreq': 'monthly'},
+        {'loc': f"{site_url}/contact/", 'priority': '0.7', 'changefreq': 'monthly'},
+        {'loc': f"{site_url}/admission-form/", 'priority': '0.9', 'changefreq': 'weekly'},
+        {'loc': f"{site_url}/library-availability/", 'priority': '0.9', 'changefreq': 'daily'},
+        {'loc': f"{site_url}/courses/", 'priority': '0.9', 'changefreq': 'weekly'},
+        {'loc': f"{site_url}/hall-of-fame/", 'priority': '0.8', 'changefreq': 'weekly'},
+        {'loc': f"{site_url}/resolved-complaints/", 'priority': '0.6', 'changefreq': 'weekly'},
+        {'loc': f"{site_url}/login/", 'priority': '0.5', 'changefreq': 'monthly'},
+        {'loc': f"{site_url}/register/", 'priority': '0.5', 'changefreq': 'monthly'},
+    ]
+
+    # Dynamic active courses
+    dynamic_urls = []
+    try:
+        courses = Course.objects.filter(is_active=True).values_list('id', flat=True)
+        for c_id in courses:
+            dynamic_urls.append({
+                'loc': f"{site_url}/courses/{c_id}/",
+                'priority': '0.8',
+                'changefreq': 'weekly'
+            })
+    except Exception:
+        pass
+
+    all_urls = static_urls + dynamic_urls
+
+    xml_lines = [
+        '<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>',
+        '<urlset xmlns="http://www.sitemaps.org/schemas/sitemap/0.9">',
+    ]
+
+    for item in all_urls:
+        xml_lines.append('  <url>')
+        xml_lines.append(f"    <loc>{item['loc']}</loc>")
+        xml_lines.append(f"    <lastmod>{now_str}</lastmod>")
+        xml_lines.append(f"    <changefreq>{item['changefreq']}</changefreq>")
+        xml_lines.append(f"    <priority>{item['priority']}</priority>")
+        xml_lines.append('  </url>')
+
+    xml_lines.append('</urlset>')
+
+    return HttpResponse('\n'.join(xml_lines), content_type="application/xml")
+
+
+

2: 
3: # 🏛️ ABCD Coaching & Smart Library Management Platform
4: ### *An Enterprise-Grade, Real-Time Educational ERP & Digital Library Ecosystem*
5: 
6: [![Live Demo](https://img.shields.io/badge/Live%20Platform-abcd2013.online-00C853?style=for-the-badge&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)](https://abcd2013.online)
7: [![Python](https://img.shields.io/badge/Python-3.12+-3776AB?style=for-the-badge&logo=python&logoColor=white)](https://python.org)
8: [![Django](https://img.shields.io/badge/Django-5.2+-092E20?style=for-the-badge&logo=django&logoColor=white)](https://djangoproject.com)
9: [![Channels](https://img.shields.io/badge/WebSockets-Django_Channels_4.3-blueviolet?style=for-the-badge&logo=socketdotio&logoColor=white)](https://channels.readthedocs.io)
10: [![Daphne](https://img.shields.io/badge/ASGI%20Server-Daphne-4B8BBE?style=for-the-badge)](https://github.com/django/daphne)
11: [![PostgreSQL](https://img.shields.io/badge/Database-PostgreSQL%20%7C%20SQLite-336791?style=for-the-badge&logo=postgresql&logoColor=white)](https://postgresql.org)
12: [![Meta WhatsApp API](https://img.shields.io/badge/WhatsApp%20Cloud%20API-Meta-25D366?style=for-the-badge&logo=whatsapp&logoColor=white)](https://developers.facebook.com/docs/whatsapp)
13: [![License](https://img.shields.io/badge/License-MIT-blue?style=for-the-badge)](LICENSE)
14: 
15: <br/>
16: 
17: <p align="center">
18:   <b>A comprehensive, state-of-the-art Web application engineered for modern educational institutes and 24/7 digital libraries.</b><br/>
19:   Featuring real-time mentorship chat, interactive visual seating allocation, automated accounting with PDF receipts, omnichannel notifications, YouTube playlist course synchronization, and autonomous background maintenance daemons.
20: </p>
21: 
22: [Explore Features](#-key-architectural-modules) • [Live Demo](#-live-deployment) • [Architecture](#-system-architecture) • [Quick Start](#-getting-started) • [Deployment](#-production-deployment)
23: 
24: ---
25: 
26: </div>
27: 
28: ## 🌟 Highlights & Achievements
29: - ⏱️ **1 Year of Continuous Engineering:** Built from the ground up with robust Django architecture, scalable ASGI WebSockets, and modular services.
30: - ⚡ **Real-Time Communication:** Sub-second latency messaging and instant push alerts powered by Django Channels and Daphne.
31: - 🪑 **Visual Seating Matrix:** Real-time interactive seat grid with 3-day grace hold period automations and instant switch workflows.
32: - 🧾 **Financially Audited:** Automated fee lifecycle tracker with cryptographic PDF receipt generator and digital verification.
33: - 📱 **Omnichannel Outreach:** Native multi-channel broadcasting via Web Push (VAPID), Meta WhatsApp Cloud API, SMTP Email, and SMS.
34: 
35: ---
36: 
37: ## 🚀 Key Architectural Modules
38: 
39: ### 1. 🎓 Guidy: Real-Time Mentorship & Communication Engine
40: *A private, high-performance messaging platform connecting students, teachers, and alumni.*
41: - **Triple-Channel Communication:** Supports **1-to-1 Direct Chats**, **Student-to-Alumni Guidance Channels**, and **Moderated Group Discussions**.
42: - **WebSocket Driven:** Instant message delivery, online presence tracking, and live typing indicators with zero-polling overhead.
43: - **Smart Message Controls:** Search message history, pin important announcements, star favorite messages, and block abusive users.
44: - **Self-Cleaning Media Lifecycle:** Automated background purger that automatically recycles expired chat media after 10 days and prunes deleted group rooms after 30 days to optimize disk usage.
45: 
46: ### 2. 🪑 Smart Library Seating Allocation & Reservation Matrix
47: *An interactive visual floor plan eliminating manual seat booking confusion.*
48: - **Interactive SVG Seating Grid:** Real-time color-coded seat visualization (Available, Occupied, Reserved, On-Hold, Locked).
49: - **Shift Management:** Multi-shift support (Morning, Afternoon, Evening, Full Day) with separate occupancy tracking.
50: - **3-Day Grace Hold & Auto-Promotions:** Automated waitlist engine that holds seats during renewal grace periods and automatically promotes waitlisted applicants.

## 🌟 Part 1: Powerful & Attractive GitHub Repository Names

Instead of a generic name like `abcd-web-platform`, choose a name that immediately conveys innovation, full-stack mastery, and enterprise architecture to anyone visiting your GitHub profile.

### 🏛️ Category A: Premium Enterprise & Campus ERP *(Recommended for Portfolio & Resume)*

### ⚡ Category B: Modern Tech & Real-Time Product *(Highlights Tech Innovation)*

---

## 🌐 Part 2: Best Domain Name Suggestions

### ⚠️ Why Upgrading from `.online` to `.in` or `.com` is a Big Win:
- **Trust & Credibility:** In India, students and parents trust **`.in`**, **`.co.in`**, or **`.com`** far more than generic new extensions like `.online`.
- **Search Engine Ranking (SEO):** Google gives strong local geotargeting priority to **`.in`** domains for Indian searches.
- **Short & Easy to Type:** Easier for students to type and remember on mobile phones.

---

### 🏆 Top Domain Name Picks to Purchase:

| Domain Option | Why It’s Great | Best Extension |
| :--- | :--- | :---: |
| **`abcdclasses.in`** | Direct, highly trusted in India for coaching institutes. | `.in` / `.com` |
| **`abcdacademy.in`** | Prestigious, academic, and covers coaching + courses. | `.in` / `.co.in` |
| **`abcdstudyhub.in`** / **`abcdstudyhub.com`** | **(Top Recommendation)** Perfectly combines both **Coaching** and the **24/7 Smart Library**. | `.in` / `.com` |
| **`abcdcampus.in`** | Clean, modern, sounds like a comprehensive digital institute. | `.in` / `.live` |
| **`myabcd.in`** / **`theabcd.in`** | Ultra-short, catchy, and brandable. | `.in` |
| **`abcdlibrary.in`** | Dedicated focus on the smart digital library service. | `.in` |
| **`studyatabcd.com`** / **`studyatabcd.in`** | Action-oriented, very popular format for modern institutes. | `.com` / `.in` |
| **`abcdeducation.in`** | Formal, authoritative, and institutional. | `.in` |

---

### 🎯 How to Pick & Price:
1. **Budget-Friendly:** `.in` domains typically cost only **₹400 – ₹500/year** on Namecheap, Cloudflare, or GoDaddy.
2. **Top Recommendation:** **`abcdstudyhub.in`** or **`abcdclasses.in`**.

---

### 🔄 What Happens When You Switch Domains in 55 Days?
Because we already made your Django configuration dynamic, changing the domain takes **less than 2 minutes**:
1. Add the new domain in your hosting dashboard (Render / Railway / VPS).
2. Update `ALLOWED_HOSTS`, `CSRF_TRUSTED_ORIGINS`, and `SITE_URL` in your environment variables.
3. Everything (SEO sitemap, robots.txt, Google Auth redirects) will automatically update!

---

### 🚀 Updated Terminal Command with Your Chosen Repo Name:

```powershell
git branch -M main
git remote add origin https://github.com/Vikas003dangi/abcd-smart-campus.git
git push -u origin main
```

**There is technically *nothing wrong* with `.online`!** 

Your current domain **`abcd2013.online` will work 100% perfectly** — it supports HTTPS (SSL), real-time WebSockets, Daphne, Google Login, email, and everything in your project without any technical issues.

---
